Distribution of model confidence across all bypass candidates. A right-skewed distribution means the ensemble is decisive about its predictions.

Empirical CDF of predicted bypass probabilities across all candidates. A steep right-side curve means most predictions are clustered at high probability — many likely-bypassing payloads.
